# C / waylib-shared 适配：feat: remove qwlroots dependency, use wlroots C API directly

- Treeland 来源：` 9582881ddc2e71c73f01eb1f8f5efd0b87a8b107 `。
- 本仓目标：` 9e58c1f7a8e4af110340e8ada6d24398d2f90c1a `。
- 原同步目标：` 93d893187e4bf4491a089d567e31a7a7da532c00 `；本页文件名采用整理后的完整目标 SHA。
- 节点：N5；action=adapted；content_action=adapted。
- [本仓总记录](../summary.md#entry-27)。

## 比较口径

比较来源唯一父提交 → 来源提交，与原目标唯一父提交 → 原目标提交的逐路径增量。
零上下文补丁对照会呈现基线位置、既有命名和本次实现差异；它不是整棵来源树与目标树的长期差异，也不声称所有显示行都是本次新增适配。
改写后的普通文件与原目标相同；派生 gitlink 在总记录单列，不混进源码适配。原验收只适用于总记录列出的原节点候选。

## 已审说明

以下保留原证据文字；其中依赖 SHA 属于原运行，新依赖身份见下文与总记录。

- ` 完成已授权原生迁移及真实 pkg-config 消费检查；新增私有依赖安装仅排除无现存类声明或调用方的 WSurfaceHandler 陈旧实现头，不补造兼容接口，其他公共目标、命名空间和有效头保持完整。 `
- ` Includes wlroots submodule update to c254362a1f79c88d21c773e22373ebe538194169. `

--    endif()
+-add_subdirectory(src)
+-

+-enable_testing()
+-add_subdirectory(tests)
 -endif()

--    # Use qwlroots from project root directory

+-  # Use qwlroots from project root directory

+-	  # 确保从父目录传递的构建选项生效
+-	  include(${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/../qwlroots/cmake/WaylandScannerHelpers.cmake)
+-	  add_subdirectory(${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/../qwlroots qwlroots)
+-	  message("Using QWlroots from submodule")
 -else()
--    find_package(QWlroots REQUIRED)
+-  find_package(WaylibSharedQWlroots REQUIRED)
 +include(${CMAKE_CURRENT_LIST_DIR}/cmake/WaylandScannerHelpers.cmake)
 +
 +# Standalone build (e.g. waylib CI packaging): the vendored wlroots tree

-+        "WaylibServer: neither the exported target Waylib::waylib-wlroots "
++        "WaylibServer: neither the exported target WaylibShared::waylib-wlroots "
 +        "nor the pkg-config package waylib-wlroots is available. Reinstall "
 +        "with the wlroots headers (include/wlr) and waylib-wlroots library "
 +        "present.")
 +endif()
-@@ -9,0 +33 @@ check_required_components(WaylibServer)

--- 来源的本次增量
+++ 本仓的本次增量
@@ -1,11 +1,24 @@
-@@ -444,0 +445,2 @@ target_include_directories(waylib-wlroots
+@@ -24,0 +25,5 @@ set(WLR_NATIVE_VERSION_PATCH 3)
++set(WLROOTS_VERSION ${WLR_NATIVE_VERSION} CACHE INTERNAL "wlroots version")
++set(WLROOTS_VERSION_MAJOR ${WLR_NATIVE_VERSION_MAJOR} CACHE INTERNAL "wlroots version_major")
++set(WLROOTS_VERSION_MINOR ${WLR_NATIVE_VERSION_MINOR} CACHE INTERNAL "wlroots version_minor")
++set(WLROOTS_VERSION_PATCH ${WLR_NATIVE_VERSION_PATCH} CACHE INTERNAL "wlroots version_patch")
++
+@@ -359 +364,2 @@ set_target_properties(waylib_wlroots_native_library PROPERTIES
+-    OUTPUT_NAME wlroots-0.19
++    OUTPUT_NAME waylib-wlroots
++    EXPORT_NAME waylib-wlroots
+@@ -376,0 +383,2 @@ target_include_directories(waylib_wlroots_native_library
 +        $<BUILD_INTERFACE:${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/protocol>
 +        $<INSTALL_INTERFACE:${CMAKE_INSTALL_INCLUDEDIR}>
-@@ -533,0 +536 @@ install(TARGETS waylib-wlroots
-+    EXPORT WaylibServerTargets
-@@ -538,0 +542,37 @@ install(TARGETS waylib-wlroots
+@@ -432 +440 @@ set_target_properties(waylib_wlroots_native_library PROPERTIES
+-# Install library only (no headers, no .pc)
++# Install native library, public headers and package metadata
+@@ -434,0 +443 @@ install(TARGETS waylib_wlroots_native_library
++    EXPORT WaylibSharedServerTargets
+@@ -439,0 +449,37 @@ install(TARGETS waylib_wlroots_native_library
 +# Install the wlroots headers so consumers of the exported
-+# Waylib::waylib-wlroots target can compile against them.
++# WaylibShared::waylib-wlroots target can compile against them.
 +install(DIRECTORY ${WLROOTS_SOURCE_DIR}/include/wlr
 +        DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_INCLUDEDIR})
 +install(DIRECTORY ${WLR_GENERATED_INCLUDE_DIR}/wlr
@@ -41,3 +54,31 @@
 +install(FILES ${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/waylib-wlroots.pc
 +        DESTINATION ${CMAKE_INSTALL_LIBDIR}/pkgconfig)
 +
+@@ -441 +487 @@ install(TARGETS waylib_wlroots_native_library
+-# Export version / feature vars for qwlroots (lowercase true/false)
++# Export native version / feature vars for Waylib (lowercase true/false)
+@@ -462 +507,0 @@ wlr_export_bool(WLR_HAVE_UDMABUF_ALLOCATOR ${WLR_HAS_UDMABUF_ALLOCATOR})
+-# qwlroots 从本次生成的 pkg-config 元数据读取版本与能力，不覆盖其公共变量。
+@@ -470,22 +514,0 @@ message(STATUS "waylib_wlroots_native_library ${WLR_NATIVE_VERSION}: "
+-
+-# 保持既有 PkgConfig::WLROOTS 公共依赖；元数据和链接均绑定当前 R。
+-set(_wlroots_pc_dir "${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/pkgconfig")
+-file(MAKE_DIRECTORY "${_wlroots_pc_dir}")
+-set(_wlroots_pc "prefix=${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}\n")
+-foreach(_feature IN ITEMS DRM_BACKEND LIBINPUT_BACKEND X11_BACKEND GLES2_RENDERER
+-        VULKAN_RENDERER GBM_ALLOCATOR UDMABUF_ALLOCATOR XWAYLAND SESSION COLOR_MANAGEMENT)
+-    string(TOLOWER "${_feature}" _feature_lower)
+-    string(APPEND _wlroots_pc "have_${_feature_lower}=${WLR_HAVE_${_feature}}\n")
+-endforeach()
+-string(APPEND _wlroots_pc "Name: wlroots-0.19\nDescription: Local wlroots library\n"
+-    "Version: ${WLR_NATIVE_VERSION}\nLibs: -L${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR} -lwlroots-0.19\n"
+-    "Cflags: -I${WLROOTS_SOURCE_DIR}/include -I${WLR_GENERATED_INCLUDE_DIR}\n")
+-file(WRITE "${_wlroots_pc_dir}/wlroots-0.19.pc" "${_wlroots_pc}")
+-set(ENV{PKG_CONFIG_PATH} "${_wlroots_pc_dir}:$ENV{PKG_CONFIG_PATH}")
+-pkg_check_modules(WLROOTS REQUIRED IMPORTED_TARGET GLOBAL wlroots-0.19>=0.19.0)
+-pkg_get_variable(_wlroots_pc_actual wlroots-0.19 pcfiledir)
+-if(NOT _wlroots_pc_actual STREQUAL _wlroots_pc_dir)
+-    message(FATAL_ERROR "wlroots metadata does not belong to the current wrapper build")
+-endif()
+-set_property(TARGET PkgConfig::WLROOTS PROPERTY
+-    INTERFACE_LINK_LIBRARIES waylib_wlroots_native_library)
```

+-find_dependency(WaylibSharedQWlroots REQUIRED)
+-
+-# WXWayland is part of the installed public API.
+-find_dependency(PkgConfig REQUIRED)
+-pkg_check_modules(XCB REQUIRED IMPORTED_TARGET xcb)
+-
+-# 包含导出的目标
+-include("${CMAKE_CURRENT_LIST_DIR}/WaylibSharedServerTargets.cmake")
+-
+-# 检查目标是否存在
+-if(NOT TARGET WaylibShared::SharedServer)
+-    set(WaylibSharedServer_FOUND FALSE)
+-    set(WaylibSharedServer_NOT_FOUND_MESSAGE "WaylibShared::SharedServer target not found")
+-    return()
+-endif()
+-
+-set(WaylibSharedServer_FOUND TRUE)
```
